function isCommunityRoom(){ return !!(state && state.meta && state.meta.eventAuthority==="community"); }
function isOwnTesterSandboxRoom(st=state){
  const uid=currentAuthUid(),m=st&&st.meta||{};
  if(!isTester()||!uid||!st) return false;
  const owned=st.createdBy===uid||st.ownerUid===uid;
  const tagged=st.testMode===true||st.testLabel==="（測試）"||st.createdByRole==="tester"||m.eventAuthority==="test";
  return !!(owned&&tagged&&m.ladderMode!=="ranked");
}
function isCommunityRoomOwner(){ return !!(isCommunityRoom() && firebaseUser && state.ownerUid===firebaseUser.uid && (currentRole==="player" || currentRole==="tester")); }
function isTester(){ return !!(userProfile && userProfile.active!==false && (userProfile.role==="tester"||userProfile.isTestAccount===true)); }
function isTestTournament(st=state){ return !!(st && st.testMode===true && st.meta && st.meta.eventAuthority==="test"); }
function isOwnTestTournament(st=state){
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  return !!(isOwnTesterSandboxRoom(st) && uid && (st.createdBy===uid||st.ownerUid===uid));
}
function ensureTestName(name){
  const clean=String(name||"BXH 封測賽事").trim().replace(/\s*（測試）\s*$/u,"");
  return clean+"（測試）";
}
function markTesterSandboxState(st){
  if(!isTester() || !st) return st;
  const uid=currentAuthUid(), created=Number(st.testCreatedAt||st.createdAt||Date.now());
  st.meta=st.meta||{};
  st.meta.eventAuthority="test";
  st.meta.ladderMode="general";
  if(typeof st.testLadderEnabled!=="boolean") st.testLadderEnabled=false;
  st.meta.name=ensureTestName(st.meta.name);
  st.meta.assignedStaffUids=uid?[uid]:[];
  st.testMode=true;
  st.testCreatedAt=created;
  st.testExpiresAtMs=created+TEST_DATA_TTL_MS;
  st.expiresAtMs=st.testExpiresAtMs;
  st.createdBy=uid;
  st.ownerUid=uid;
  st.createdByRole="tester";
  st.ladderPointsAwarded=false;
  return st;
}
function testSandboxBannerHtml(st=state){
  if(!isTestTournament(st)) return "";
  const expires=Number(st.testExpiresAtMs||st.expiresAtMs||0);
  const label=expires?new Date(expires).toLocaleString():"建立後 7 天";
  return `<div class="banner warn test-sandbox-banner"><span><b>封測沙盒（測試）</b>｜所有新增與操作皆為測試資料，不會進入正式天梯；預計 ${esc(label)} 自動清除。</span></div>`;
}

function refereeStationAssignmentsMap(st=state){
  const m=st&&st.meta&&st.meta.refereeStationAssignments;
  return (m&&typeof m==="object"&&!Array.isArray(m))?m:{};
}
function refereeStationNamesMap(st=state){

  return (m&&typeof m==="object"&&!Array.isArray(m))?m:{};

function refereeStationRestrictionEnabled(st=state){ return !!(st&&st.meta&&st.meta.refereeStationRestrictionEnabled); }
function refereeUidsForStation(stationNum, st=state){

  const raw=map[String(Number(stationNum)||1)] || [];

}
function refereeNamesForStation(stationNum, st=state){

}
function refereeDisplayForStation(stationNum, st=state){
  const names=refereeNamesForStation(stationNum,st);
  return names.length?names.join("、"):"未指定";
}
function canOperateStation(stationNum, st=state){
  if(isCommunityRoomOwner()) return true;
  if(isOwnTestTournament(st)) return true;
  if(isTester()) return false;
  if(isAdminTierOrAbove()) return true;
  if(!isStaffTier()) return hasAdminAccess();
  if(!refereeStationRestrictionEnabled(st)) return true;
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  return !!(uid && refereeUidsForStation(stationNum,st).includes(uid));
}
function canOperateMatchStation(matchOrId, st=state){
  const m=typeof matchOrId==="string" ? ((st&&st.matches)||[]).find(x=>x.id===matchOrId) : matchOrId;
  return !!(m && canOperateStation(m.station,st));
}
function requireStationPermission(matchOrId, st=state){
  if(st&&st.archiveStatus==="completed"){ showToast("此賽事已完成並封存，目前為唯讀。",true); return false; }
  const m=typeof matchOrId==="string" ? ((st&&st.matches)||[]).find(x=>x.id===matchOrId) : matchOrId;
  if(!canOperateMatchStation(m,st)){
    showToast(`此戰鬥台未指派給你${m&&m.station?`（Court ${m.station}）`:""}，目前僅可查看。`, true);
    return false;

function canOperateMatchStation(matchOrId, st=state){

function requireStationPermission(matchOrId, st=state){
  if(st&&st.archiveStatus==="completed"){ showToast("此賽事已完成並封存，目前為唯讀。",true); return false; }
  const m=typeof matchOrId==="string" ? ((st&&st.matches)||[]).find(x=>x.id===matchOrId) : matchOrId;
  if(!canOperateMatchStation(m,st)){
    showToast(`此戰鬥台未指派給你${m&&m.station?`（Court ${m.station}）`:""}，目前僅可查看。`, true);
    return false;
  }
  if(st.cloudCode && (!cloudAvailable() || cloudStatus!=="connected")){
    showToast("目前雲端尚未同步完成；為避免覆蓋其他裁判的比分或場次安排，本台暫時唯讀。", true);
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}
function flattenedRefereeStationUids(st=state){
  const out=[];
  Object.values(refereeStationAssignmentsMap(st)).forEach(list=>{ if(Array.isArray(list)) list.forEach(uid=>{ if(uid&&!out.includes(uid)) out.push(uid); }); });
  return out;
}
// v13.28.0: when Court responsibility is enabled, multiple referee devices may
// score different courts at the same time. A normal pushUpdate writes the WHOLE
// tournament JSON and can therefore overwrite another court with a stale copy.
// Restricted staff match mutations use Firestore transactions instead: each
// transaction re-reads the newest cloud state, changes only the intended match /
// same-court queue fields, and commits the merged latest state.

function shouldUseStationScopedMutation(matchOrId){
  const m=typeof matchOrId==="string"?getMatch(matchOrId):matchOrId;
  return !!(m && state.cloudCode && cloudAvailable()
    && window.cloudSync && typeof window.cloudSync.mutateMatchTransaction==="function");
}

function requireStationPermission(matchOrId, st=state){
  if(st&&st.archiveStatus==="completed"){ showToast("此賽事已完成並封存，目前為唯讀。",true); return false; }
  const m=typeof matchOrId==="string" ? ((st&&st.matches)||[]).find(x=>x.id===matchOrId) : matchOrId;
  if(!canOperateMatchStation(m,st)){
    showToast(`此戰鬥台未指派給你${m&&m.station?`（Court ${m.station}）`:""}，目前僅可查看。`, true);
    return false;
  }
  if(st.cloudCode && (!cloudAvailable() || cloudStatus!=="connected")){
    showToast("目前雲端尚未同步完成；為避免覆蓋其他裁判的比分或場次安排，本台暫時唯讀。", true);
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}
function flattenedRefereeStationUids(st=state){

// v13.28.0: when Court responsibility is enabled, multiple referee devices may
// score different courts at the same time. A normal pushUpdate writes the WHOLE
// tournament JSON and can therefore overwrite another court with a stale copy.
// Restricted staff match mutations use Firestore transactions instead: each
// transaction re-reads the newest cloud state, changes only the intended match /
// same-court queue fields, and commits the merged latest state.
let stationMutationChain = Promise.resolve({ok:true});

    && window.cloudSync && typeof window.cloudSync.mutateMatchTransaction==="function");
}

    "already-completed":"本場已由其他裁判完成判定。",
    "invalid-score":"雲端比分已變更，請重新整理後再操作。",

  };

function canOperateStation(stationNum, st=state){
  if(isCommunityRoomOwner()) return true;
  if(isOwnTestTournament(st)) return true;
  if(isTester()) return false;
  if(isAdminTierOrAbove()) return true;
  if(!isStaffTier()) return hasAdminAccess();
  if(!refereeStationRestrictionEnabled(st)) return true;
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  return !!(uid && refereeUidsForStation(stationNum,st).includes(uid));
}

  const m=typeof matchOrId==="string" ? ((st&&st.matches)||[]).find(x=>x.id===matchOrId) : matchOrId;
  return !!(m && canOperateStation(m.station,st));
}
function requireStationPermission(matchOrId, st=state){
  if(st&&st.archiveStatus==="completed"){ showToast("此賽事已完成並封存，目前為唯讀。",true); return false; }

    showToast(`此戰鬥台未指派給你${m&&m.station?`（Court ${m.station}）`:""}，目前僅可查看。`, true);
    return false;
  }
  if(st.cloudCode && (!cloudAvailable() || cloudStatus!=="connected")){
    showToast("目前雲端尚未同步完成；為避免覆蓋其他裁判的比分或場次安排，本台暫時唯讀。", true);
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

async function ensureCloudAvailableForArchive(){
  if(cloudAvailable()) return true;
  if(!(typeof window!=="undefined" && window.cloudSync)) return false;
  try{
    if(typeof window.cloudSync.connect==="function") await window.cloudSync.connect();
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("[archive] cloud reconnect failed", e);
  }
  return cloudAvailable();
}

async function writeArchiveStateToCloud(snapshot){
  if(!snapshot || !snapshot.cloudCode) return false;
  const ready = await ensureCloudAvailableForArchive();
  if(!ready || !window.cloudSync || typeof window.cloudSync.pushUpdate!=="function") return false;
  try{
    await flushCloudStateWrites();
    return await window.cloudSync.pushUpdate(snapshot.cloudCode, snapshot) === true;
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("[archive] direct cloud fallback failed", e);
    return false;
  }
}

// Idempotent: safe to call multiple times for the same tournament (re-opened celebration
// screen, page refresh, remote-state re-application) — only performs the archive write once.
async function performAutoArchive(){
  if(state.archiveStatus==="completed" && state.archivedAt) return true;

  state.settlementAttempt=Number(state.settlementAttempt||0)+1;
  state.settlementPhase="processing";
  state.settlementStartedAt=state.settlementStartedAt||Date.now();
  state.settlementError=null;
  state.settlementErrorCode=null;
  state.updatedAt=Date.now();
  try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}

  // Tester ranked rooms use a completely isolated test ladder. Official ladderMode stays general.
  if(isOwnTestTournament() && state.testLadderEnabled===true){
    state.settlementPhase="ladder";
    try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
    if(!state.cloudCode || !cloudAvailable() || !window.cloudSync || !window.cloudSync.settleTestLadderTournament){
      showToast("測試積分賽必須連上 Firebase 才能完成測試天梯結算", true);
      return false;
    }
    let payload;
    try{ payload=buildLadderSettlementPayload(state); }
    catch(e){ showToast(e.message||'V1 積分資料驗證失敗',true); return false; }
    const requiredTop=Math.min(4,payload.participants.length);
    const topCount=payload.participants.filter(x=>x.placement&&x.placement<=4).length;
    if(topCount<requiredTop){ showToast("測試天梯結算失敗：前四名資料尚未完整。",true); return false; }
    try{
      await flushCloudStateWrites();
      const settled=await window.cloudSync.settleTestLadderTournament(state.cloudCode,payload);
      if(!settled||!settled.ok){ state.settlementError=(settled&&settled.message)||"測試天梯積分寫入失敗";await saveRecord(state);showToast(state.settlementError,true); return false; }
      state.testLadderPointsAwarded=settled.skipped!==true;
      state.testLadderSettlementStatus=settled.skipped?"skipped":"awarded";
      state.testLadderSettlementNote=settled.message||"";
      state.testLadderSkippedPlayers=settled.skippedPlayers||[];
      state.testLadderSeasonId="TEST-S1";
      state.testLadderAwardedAt=settled.awardedAt||Date.now();
      state.testLadderResults=settled.results||[];
      showToast(settled.skipped?settled.message:settled.alreadyAwarded?"本場測試天梯先前已完成結算，不會重複加分":"測試天梯積分結算完成");
    }catch(e){ console.error("[test ladder settlement]",e); state.settlementError="測試天梯結算失敗；賽事尚未封存，請確認連線後重試";await saveRecord(state);showToast(state.settlementError,true); return false; }
  }

  // Ranked events are not considered completed until the database-backed
  // ladder transaction has succeeded (or the cloud proves it was already awarded).
  if(state.meta && state.meta.ladderMode==="ranked"){
    state.settlementPhase="ladder";
    try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
    if(!isAdminTierOrAbove()){
      state.settlementError="積分賽事需由管理員／最高管理員完成天梯結算後才能封存";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-permission";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    if(!state.cloudCode || !cloudAvailable() || !window.cloudSync || !window.cloudSync.settleLadderTournament){
      state.settlementError="積分賽事必須連上 Firebase 才能完成；目前未結算，因此不會封存";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-cloud-unavailable";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    let payload;
    try{ payload=buildLadderSettlementPayload(state); }
    catch(e){ showToast(e.message||'V1 積分資料驗證失敗',true); return false; }
    const requiredTop=Math.min(4,payload.participants.length);
    const topCount=payload.participants.filter(x=>x.placement&&x.placement<=4).length;
    if(topCount<requiredTop){

      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-incomplete";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);

        state.settlementError=msg;

        state.settlementPhase="failed";
        await saveRecord(state);
        showToast(msg,true);
        return false;

      state.ladderSeasonId=settled.seasonId||null;
      state.ladderAwardedAt=settled.awardedAt||Date.now();

      state.ladderResults=Array.isArray(settled.results)?settled.results:[];

function datetimeLocalToEpoch(str){
  if(!str) return null;
  const t = new Date(str).getTime();
  return isNaN(t) ? null : t;
}

function renderStaffAssignmentPanel(){
  const assigned = state.meta.assignedStaffUids || [];
  if(!isAdminTierOrAbove()){
    return ""; // staff themselves don't manage assignment
  }
  if(accountMgmtUsers===null){
    return `<div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-title">被分派的工作人員</div>
      <p class="hint">尚未載入工作人員清單。</p>
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" data-action="load-staff-assignment-list">載入工作人員清單</button>
    </div>`;
  }
  const candidates = accountMgmtUsers.filter(u=>u.role==="staff" || u.role==="admin");
  if(candidates.length===0){
    return `<div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-title">被分派的工作人員</div>
      <p class="hint">目前沒有 staff 或 admin 帳號可供指派。未指派任何人時，所有在職 staff／admin 皆可操作此賽事。</p>
    </div>`;
  }
  return `<div class="panel">
    <div class="panel-title">被分派的工作人員</div>
    <p class="hint">未勾選任何人時，所有在職 staff／admin 皆可操作此賽事（過渡期預設）；勾選後，只有勾選的人可以操作此賽事的資料。</p>
    <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-top:10px;">
      ${candidates.map(u=>`
        <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:13px;">
          <input type="checkbox" class="staff-assign-checkbox" data-uid="${esc(u.uid)}" ${assigned.includes(u.uid)?'checked':''} style="width:18px;height:18px;">
          ${esc(u.displayName||u.email)}　<span class="hint" style="margin:0;">(${u.role==="admin"?"管理員":"工作人員"})</span>
        </label>`).join("")}
    </div>
    <div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:12px;">
      <button class="btn btn-primary" data-action="save-staff-assignment">儲存分派名單</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

  return `<div class="panel"><div class="panel-title">裁判台責任綁定</div><p class="hint">建議賽前完成分配。啟用後，未被指定的 staff 在其他 Court 只可查看，不能計分、確認結果、簡易判定、暫停、跳過或重賽。玩家端只會看到裁判顯示名稱，不公開 UID。</p>${directoryHint}${refereeDirectoryError?`<div class="auth-error">${esc(refereeDirectoryError)}</div>`:""}<div class="toggle-row" style="margin:12px 0;"><span><b>啟用桌次限制</b><small>未啟用時沿用既有賽事權限；啟用後依 Court 指派限制。</small></span><button class="toggle ${enabled?'on':''}" data-action="toggle-referee-station-restriction" data-value="${enabled?'false':'true'}" ${refereeAssignmentSaving?'disabled':''}><span></span></button></div>${draftHint}<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;">${rows}</div><div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:12px;"><button class="btn btn-primary" data-action="save-referee-station-assignment" ${refereeAssignmentSaving?'disabled':''}>${refereeAssignmentSaving?'雲端交易確認中…':'儲存裁判台分配'}</button><button class="btn btn-ghost" data-action="load-referee-directory" ${refereeAssignmentSaving?'disabled':''}>重新載入名單</button></div></div>`;

    async getLadderSnapshot(){

      ]);

    async getLadderSnapshot(){

      // Previously these were serial, followed by another season read, causing
      // avoidable latency on mobile even with only one ladder player.

      ]);

      if(control&&control.currentSeason){
        const ss=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSeasons",control.currentSeason));
        if(ss.exists()) season=ss.data();
      }
      return {control,season,players};
    },

/* ==== registration settings draft (Phase 3 fix) ====
   Fixes a real bug: clicking any of the quick-toggle buttons on the
   registration settings panel (waitlist on/off, public/private, target
   group, cancel event) used to mutate state.meta directly and re-render
   immediately — which discarded anything the admin had typed into the other
   fields (open/close time, capacity, fee, description, notes) but not yet
   saved. Now EVERY field on this panel — including the raw text/number/
   datetime inputs — is backed by this draft object, and nothing touches
   state.meta / saveState() / Firebase until "儲存報名設定" is explicitly
   clicked and validation passes. */
let registrationFormDraft = null;
let registrationFormDraftDirty = false;

/* v13.20.0 — unified tournament settings draft.
   All visible fields in the main "賽事設定" workflow are backed by this
   object. UI actions that re-render the page (ladder mode, scoring mode,
   registration mode, check-in mode, round mode) first sync DOM -> draft,
   so unfinished edits never disappear. Firebase/state.meta is only committed
   by the explicit "儲存賽事設定" action, except registration/check-in mode
   choices that already had intentional immediate side-effects. */
let settingsFormDraft = null;
let settingsFormDraftDirty = false;

function ensureSettingsFormDraft(){
  if(settingsFormDraft) return;
  const m=state.meta||{};
  settingsFormDraft={
    name:m.name||"",
    date:m.date||"",
    location:m.location||"",
    checkin:m.checkin||"",
    startTime:m.startTime||"",
    formatType:m.formatType||"single",
    stations:Math.max(1,parseInt(m.stations,10)||1),
    bronzeMatch:!!m.bronzeMatch,
    ladderMode:(isTester()&&state.testLadderEnabled===true)?"ranked":(m.ladderMode==="ranked"?"ranked":"general"),
    scoringMode:m.scoringMode==="quick"?"quick":"standard",
    checkinRequired:!!m.checkinRequired,
    checkinLeadMinutes:(m.checkinLeadMinutes===30?30:60),
  };
  settingsFormDraftDirty=false;
}

function resetSettingsFormDraft(){
  settingsFormDraft=null;
  settingsFormDraftDirty=false;
}

function syncSettingsDraftFromDom(){
  if(!settingsFormDraft || typeof document==="undefined") return;
  const val=(id,fallback)=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    return el ? el.value : fallback;
  };
  settingsFormDraft.name=val("f-name",settingsFormDraft.name);
  settingsFormDraft.date=val("f-date",settingsFormDraft.date);
  settingsFormDraft.location=val("f-location",settingsFormDraft.location);
  settingsFormDraft.checkin=val("f-checkin",settingsFormDraft.checkin);
  settingsFormDraft.startTime=val("f-start",settingsFormDraft.startTime);
  settingsFormDraft.formatType=val("f-format",settingsFormDraft.formatType)||"single";

  const st=document.getElementById("f-stations");
  if(st) settingsFormDraft.stations=Math.max(1,parseInt(st.value,10)||1);

  const bronze=document.getElementById("f-bronze");
  if(bronze) settingsFormDraft.bronzeMatch=!!bronze.checked;

  const lead=document.getElementById("f-checkin-lead");
  if(lead) settingsFormDraft.checkinLeadMinutes=parseInt(lead.value,10)===30?30:60;
}

function ensureRegistrationFormDraft(){
  if(registrationFormDraft) return;
  const m = state.meta;
  registrationFormDraft = {
    registrationEnabled: !!m.registrationEnabled,
    registrationOpenAt: m.registrationOpenAt || null,
    registrationCloseAt: m.registrationCloseAt || null,
    registrationCapacity: m.registrationCapacity || null,
    registrationFee: m.registrationFee!=null ? m.registrationFee : null,
    waitlistEnabled: (Number(m.waitlistCapacity)||0) > 0,
    waitlistCapacity: Number(m.waitlistCapacity)||0,
    eventDescription: m.eventDescription || "",
    registrationNotes: m.registrationNotes || "",
    registrationVisibility: m.registrationVisibility || "public",
    targetGroup: m.targetGroup || "open",
    participantNameMode: m.participantNameMode==="gameId" ? "gameId" : "realName",
  };
  registrationFormDraftDirty = false;
}
function resetRegistrationFormDraft(){
  registrationFormDraft = null;
  registrationFormDraftDirty = false;
}
// Reads every field marked with data-draft-field on the currently-rendered
// registration settings panel and writes it into registrationFormDraft —
// used both by the live input/change binding (bindDynamicInputs, so nothing
// is lost even if the admin clicks a toggle mid-typing) and once more,
// belt-and-braces, right before "儲存報名設定" actually commits anything.
function syncRegistrationDraftFromDom(){
  if(!registrationFormDraft || typeof document==="undefined" || typeof document.querySelectorAll!=="function") return;
  document.querySelectorAll("[data-draft-field]").forEach(el=>{
    const field = el.getAttribute("data-draft-field");
    const type = el.getAttribute("data-draft-type");
    let value;
    if(type==="datetime"){ value = datetimeLocalToEpoch(el.value); }
    else if(type==="int"){ const n = parseInt(el.value,10); value = isNaN(n) ? null : n; }
    else if(type==="int-or-null"){ value = (el.value==="" ) ? null : (isNaN(parseInt(el.value,10)) ? null : parseInt(el.value,10)); }
    else { value = el.value; }
    registrationFormDraft[field] = value;
  });
}

function canOperateStation(stationNum, st=state){
  if(isCommunityRoomOwner()) return true;
  if(isOwnTestTournament(st)) return true;
  if(isTester()) return false;
  if(isAdminTierOrAbove()) return true;
  if(!isStaffTier()) return hasAdminAccess();
  if(!refereeStationRestrictionEnabled(st)) return true;
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  return !!(uid && refereeUidsForStation(stationNum,st).includes(uid));
}
function canOperateMatchStation(matchOrId, st=state){
  const m=typeof matchOrId==="string" ? ((st&&st.matches)||[]).find(x=>x.id===matchOrId) : matchOrId;
  return !!(m && canOperateStation(m.station,st));
}
function requireStationPermission(matchOrId, st=state){
  if(st&&st.archiveStatus==="completed"){ showToast("此賽事已完成並封存，目前為唯讀。",true); return false; }
  const m=typeof matchOrId==="string" ? ((st&&st.matches)||[]).find(x=>x.id===matchOrId) : matchOrId;
  if(!canOperateMatchStation(m,st)){
    showToast(`此戰鬥台未指派給你${m&&m.station?`（Court ${m.station}）`:""}，目前僅可查看。`, true);
    return false;
  }
  if(st.cloudCode && (!cloudAvailable() || cloudStatus!=="connected")){
    showToast("目前雲端尚未同步完成；為避免覆蓋其他裁判的比分或場次安排，本台暫時唯讀。", true);
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}
function flattenedRefereeStationUids(st=state){

  return out;
}

  "admin-load-engagement-overview","admin-update-title-definition","admin-backfill-preview","admin-backfill-execute","admin-backfill-restart","admin-engagement-diagnose"
]);
const ADMIN_TIER_ONLY_ACTIONS = new Set([
  "set-scoring-mode","set-round-scoring-mode","correct-quick-result","board-reshuffle",
  "view-changelog","download-changelog","load-staff-assignment-list","save-staff-assignment"
]);
const TESTER_TIER_ACTIONS = new Set(["set-scoring-mode","set-round-scoring-mode","correct-quick-result","board-reshuffle"]);
const TESTER_CREATION_ACTIONS = new Set(["new-tournament-blank"]);
const TOURNAMENT_LIVE_OPERATOR_ACTIONS = new Set([
  "score","undo-score","rematch","confirm-result",
  "set-match-in-progress","set-match-ready","pause-match-status","resume-match-status",
  "skip-match","claim-skipped-match","court-swap-start","open-quick-confirm","archive-complete"
]);

function hasAdminAccess(){ return currentRole === "admin" || currentRole === "tester"; }
function hasPrivilegedTournamentProfile(){
  return !!(userProfile && userProfile.active!==false && userProfile.isTestAccount!==true &&
    ["super_admin","admin","staff"].includes(userProfile.role));
}
function isCommunityRoom(){ return !!(state && state.meta && state.meta.eventAuthority==="community"); }
function isOwnTesterSandboxRoom(st=state){
  const uid=currentAuthUid(),m=st&&st.meta||{};
  if(!isTester()||!uid||!st) return false;
  const owned=st.createdBy===uid||st.ownerUid===uid;
  const tagged=st.testMode===true||st.testLabel==="（測試）"||st.createdByRole==="tester"||m.eventAuthority==="test";
  return !!(owned&&tagged&&m.ladderMode!=="ranked");
}
function isCommunityRoomOwner(){ return !!(isCommunityRoom() && firebaseUser && state.ownerUid===firebaseUser.uid && (currentRole==="player" || currentRole==="tester")); }
function isTester(){ return !!(userProfile && userProfile.active!==false && (userProfile.role==="tester"||userProfile.isTestAccount===true)); }
function isTestTournament(st=state){ return !!(st && st.testMode===true && st.meta && st.meta.eventAuthority==="test"); }
function isOwnTestTournament(st=state){
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  return !!(isOwnTesterSandboxRoom(st) && uid && (st.createdBy===uid||st.ownerUid===uid));
}
function ensureTestName(name){
  const clean=String(name||"BXH 封測賽事").trim().replace(/\s*（測試）\s*$/u,"");
  return clean+"（測試）";
}
function markTesterSandboxState(st){
  if(!isTester() || !st) return st;
  const uid=currentAuthUid(), created=Number(st.testCreatedAt||st.createdAt||Date.now());
  st.meta=st.meta||{};
  st.meta.eventAuthority="test";
  st.meta.ladderMode="general";
  if(typeof st.testLadderEnabled!=="boolean") st.testLadderEnabled=false;
  st.meta.name=ensureTestName(st.meta.name);
  st.meta.assignedStaffUids=uid?[uid]:[];
  st.testMode=true;
  st.testCreatedAt=created;
  st.testExpiresAtMs=created+TEST_DATA_TTL_MS;
  st.expiresAtMs=st.testExpiresAtMs;
  st.createdBy=uid;
  st.ownerUid=uid;
  st.createdByRole="tester";
  st.ladderPointsAwarded=false;
  return st;
}
function testSandboxBannerHtml(st=state){
  if(!isTestTournament(st)) return "";
  const expires=Number(st.testExpiresAtMs||st.expiresAtMs||0);
  const label=expires?new Date(expires).toLocaleString():"建立後 7 天";
  return `<div class="banner warn test-sandbox-banner"><span><b>封測沙盒（測試）</b>｜所有新增與操作皆為測試資料，不會進入正式天梯；預計 ${esc(label)} 自動清除。</span></div>`;
}
function canOperateCurrentTournament(){ return (hasAdminAccess() && (!isTester() || isOwnTestTournament())) || (hasPrivilegedTournamentProfile() && !isCommunityRoom()) || isCommunityRoomOwner(); }
function canConfigureCurrentTournament(){ return isAdminTierOrAbove() || isOwnTestTournament() || isCommunityRoomOwner(); }
function canJoinCloudRoom(){ return currentRole === "admin" || currentRole === "guest" || currentRole === "player"; }
function isSuperAdmin(){ return !!(userProfile && userProfile.active!==false && userProfile.isTestAccount!==true && userProfile.role==="super_admin"); }
function isAdminTierOrAbove(){ return !!(userProfile && userProfile.active!==false && !isTester() && (userProfile.role==="super_admin" || userProfile.role==="admin")); }
function isStaffTier(){ return !!(userProfile && userProfile.active!==false && !isTester() && userProfile.role==="staff"); }
function canCreateOfficialTournament(){ return !!(userProfile && userProfile.active!==false && !isTester() && (userProfile.role==="super_admin" || userProfile.role==="admin" || userProfile.role==="staff")); }
function currentAuthUid(){ return (firebaseUser&&firebaseUser.uid)||null; }
function isCurrentOfficialTournamentCreator(){
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  return !!(uid && state && state.meta && state.meta.eventAuthority!=="community" && state.createdBy===uid);
}
function canManageRefereeStationAssignments(){
  return isAdminTierOrAbove() || isOwnTestTournament() || (isStaffTier() && isCurrentOfficialTournamentCreator());
}
function refereeStationAssignmentsMap(st=state){
  const m=st&&st.meta&&st.meta.refereeStationAssignments;
  return (m&&typeof m==="object"&&!Array.isArray(m))?m:{};
}
function refereeStationNamesMap(st=state){
  const m=st&&st.meta&&st.meta.refereeStationNames;
  return (m&&typeof m==="object"&&!Array.isArray(m))?m:{};
}
function refereeStationRestrictionEnabled(st=state){ return !!(st&&st.meta&&st.meta.refereeStationRestrictionEnabled); }
function refereeUidsForStation(stationNum, st=state){
  const map=refereeStationAssignmentsMap(st);
  const raw=map[String(Number(stationNum)||1)] || [];
  return Array.isArray(raw)?raw.filter(Boolean):[];
}
function refereeNamesForStation(stationNum, st=state){
  const map=refereeStationNamesMap(st);
  const raw=map[String(Number(stationNum)||1)] || [];
  return Array.isArray(raw)?raw.filter(Boolean):[];
}
function refereeDisplayForStation(stationNum, st=state){
  const names=refereeNamesForStation(stationNum,st);
  return names.length?names.join("、"):"未指定";
}
function canOperateStation(stationNum, st=state){
  if(isCommunityRoomOwner()) return true;
  if(isOwnTestTournament(st)) return true;
  if(isTester()) return false;
  if(isAdminTierOrAbove()) return true;
  if(!isStaffTier()) return hasAdminAccess();
  if(!refereeStationRestrictionEnabled(st)) return true;
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  return !!(uid && refereeUidsForStation(stationNum,st).includes(uid));
}
function canOperateMatchStation(matchOrId, st=state){
  const m=typeof matchOrId==="string" ? ((st&&st.matches)||[]).find(x=>x.id===matchOrId) : matchOrId;
  return !!(m && canOperateStation(m.station,st));
}
function requireStationPermission(matchOrId, st=state){
  if(st&&st.archiveStatus==="completed"){ showToast("此賽事已完成並封存，目前為唯讀。",true); return false; }
  const m=typeof matchOrId==="string" ? ((st&&st.matches)||[]).find(x=>x.id===matchOrId) : matchOrId;
  if(!canOperateMatchStation(m,st)){
    showToast(`此戰鬥台未指派給你${m&&m.station?`（Court ${m.station}）`:""}，目前僅可查看。`, true);
    return false;
  }
  if(st.cloudCode && (!cloudAvailable() || cloudStatus!=="connected")){
    showToast("目前雲端尚未同步完成；為避免覆蓋其他裁判的比分或場次安排，本台暫時唯讀。", true);
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}
function flattenedRefereeStationUids(st=state){
  const out=[];
  Object.values(refereeStationAssignmentsMap(st)).forEach(list=>{ if(Array.isArray(list)) list.forEach(uid=>{ if(uid&&!out.includes(uid)) out.push(uid); }); });
  return out;
}
// v13.28.0: when Court responsibility is enabled, multiple referee devices may
// score different courts at the same time. A normal pushUpdate writes the WHOLE
// tournament JSON and can therefore overwrite another court with a stale copy.
// Restricted staff match mutations use Firestore transactions instead: each
// tr
--- match 4 @ 705666 ---
ePromise = saveRecord(snapshot);

  if(snapshot.cloudCode && cloudAvailable() && currentRole!=="guest"){
    try{
      const cloudPromise = enqueueCloudStateWrite(snapshot.cloudCode, snapshot);
      const localOk = await localSavePromise;
      const ok = await cloudPromise;
      if(ok){
        cloudSyncPending = false;
        cloudStatus = "connected";
        cloudLastSyncAt = Date.now();
        return localOk ? true : false;
      }
      const lastCode = (typeof window!=="undefined" && window.__BXH_LAST_CLOUD_ERROR_CODE) || "";
      if(lastCode==="permission-denied"){
        cloudAccessLimited = true;
        cloudAccessMessage = "此帳號沒有執行這項雲端寫入的權限。";
        cloudStatus = "connected";
      }else{
        cloudAccessLimited = false; cloudAccessMessage = ""; cloudStatus = "error";
        queueCloudSyncRetry(snapshot.id, snapshot.cloudCode);
      }
      return false;
    }catch(e){
      await localSavePromise;
      const code=(e&&e.code)||"";
      if(code==="permission-denied"){
        cloudAccessLimited=true; cloudAccessMessage="此帳號沒有執行這項雲端寫入的權限。"; cloudStatus="connected";
      }else{
        cloudAccessLimited=false; cloudAccessMessage=""; cloudStatus="error"; queueCloudSyncRetry(snapshot.id, snapshot.cloudCode);
      }
      return false;
    }
  }

  const localOk = await localSavePromise;
  return localOk; // no cloud room active; report the real local persistence result
}

async function ensureCloudAvailableForArchive(){
  if(cloudAvailable()) return true;
  if(!(typeof window!=="undefined" && window.cloudSync)) return false;
  try{
    if(typeof window.cloudSync.connect==="function") await window.cloudSync.connect();
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("[archive] cloud reconnect failed", e);
  }
  return cloudAvailable();
}

async function writeArchiveStateToCloud(snapshot){
  if(!snapshot || !snapshot.cloudCode) return false;
  const ready = await ensureCloudAvailableForArchive();
  if(!ready || !window.cloudSync || typeof window.cloudSync.pushUpdate!=="function") return false;
  try{
    await flushCloudStateWrites();
    return await window.cloudSync.pushUpdate(snapshot.cloudCode, snapshot) === true;
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("[archive] direct cloud fallback failed", e);
    return false;
  }
}

// Idempotent: safe to call multiple times for the same tournament (re-opened celebration
// screen, page refresh, remote-state re-application) — only performs the archive write once.
async function performAutoArchive(){
  if(state.archiveStatus==="completed" && state.archivedAt) return true;

  state.settlementAttempt=Number(state.settlementAttempt||0)+1;
  state.settlementPhase="processing";
  state.settlementStartedAt=state.settlementStartedAt||Date.now();
  state.settlementError=null;
  state.settlementErrorCode=null;
  state.updatedAt=Date.now();
  try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}

  // Tester ranked rooms use a completely isolated test ladder. Official ladderMode stays general.
  if(isOwnTestTournament() && state.testLadderEnabled===true){
    state.settlementPhase="ladder";
    try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
    if(!state.cloudCode || !cloudAvailable() || !window.cloudSync || !window.cloudSync.settleTestLadderTournament){
      showToast("測試積分賽必須連上 Firebase 才能完成測試天梯結算", true);
      return false;
    }
    let payload;
    try{ payload=buildLadderSettlementPayload(state); }
    catch(e){ showToast(e.message||'V1 積分資料驗證失敗',true); return false; }
    const requiredTop=Math.min(4,payload.participants.length);
    const topCount=payload.participants.filter(x=>x.placement&&x.placement<=4).length;
    if(topCount<requiredTop){ showToast("測試天梯結算失敗：前四名資料尚未完整。",true); return false; }
    try{
      await flushCloudStateWrites();
      const settled=await window.cloudSync.settleTestLadderTournament(state.cloudCode,payload);
      if(!settled||!settled.ok){ state.settlementError=(settled&&settled.message)||"測試天梯積分寫入失敗";await saveRecord(state);showToast(state.settlementError,true); return false; }
      state.testLadderPointsAwarded=settled.skipped!==true;
      state.testLadderSettlementStatus=settled.skipped?"skipped":"awarded";
      state.testLadderSettlementNote=settled.message||"";
      state.testLadderSkippedPlayers=settled.skippedPlayers||[];
      state.testLadderSeasonId="TEST-S1";
      state.testLadderAwardedAt=settled.awardedAt||Date.now();
      state.testLadderResults=settled.results||[];
      showToast(settled.skipped?settled.message:settled.alreadyAwarded?"本場測試天梯先前已完成結算，不會重複加分":"測試天梯積分結算完成");
    }catch(e){ console.error("[test ladder settlement]",e); state.settlementError="測試天梯結算失敗；賽事尚未封存，請確認連線後重試";await saveRecord(state);showToast(state.settlementError,true); return false; }
  }

  // Ranked events are not considered completed until the database-backed
  // ladder transaction has succeeded (or the cloud proves it was already awarded).
  if(state.meta && state.meta.ladderMode==="ranked"){
    state.settlementPhase="ladder";
    try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
    if(!isAdminTierOrAbove()){
      state.settlementError="積分賽事需由管理員／最高管理員完成天梯結算後才能封存";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-permission";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    if(!state.cloudCode || !cloudAvailable() || !window.cloudSync || !window.cloudSync.settleLadderTournament){
      state.settlementError="積分賽事必須連上 Firebase 才能完成；目前未結算，因此不會封存";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-cloud-unavailable";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    let payload;
    try{ payload=buildLadderSettlementPayload(state); }
    catch(e){ showToast(e.message||'V1 積分資料驗證失敗',true); return false; }
    const requiredTop=Math.min(4,payload.participants.length);
    const topCount=payload.participants.filter(x=>x.placement&&x.placement<=4).length;
    if(topCount<requiredTop){
      state.settlementError="天梯結算失敗：前四名資料尚未完整。單淘汰積分賽需完成季殿賽。";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-incomplete";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    try{
      await flushCloudStateWrites();
      const settled=await window.cloudSync.settleLadderTournament(state.cloudCode,payload);
      if(!settled || !settled.ok){
        const missing=settled&&settled.unlinkedPlayers&&settled.unlinkedPlayers.length ? "："+settled.unlinkedPlayers.join("、") : "";
        const msg=(settled&&settled.reason)==="unlinked-players" ? "以下選手無法連結唯一 BXH 玩家帳號"+missing : (settled&&settled.message)||"天梯積分寫入失敗";
        state.settlementError=msg;
        state.settlementErrorCode=(settled&&settled.reason)||"ranked-settlement-failed";
        state.settlementPhase="failed";
        await saveRecord(state);
        showToast(msg,true);
        return false;
      }
      state.ladderPointsAwarded=true;
      state.ladderSeasonId=settled.seasonId||null;
      state.ladderAwardedAt=settled.awardedAt||Date.now();
      state.ladderSkippedPlayers=Array.isArray(settled.skippedPlayers)?settled.skippedPlayers:[];
      state.ladderResults=Array.isArray(settled.results)?settled.results:[];
      ladderPublicLoaded=false; ladderAdminLogsLoaded=false; myLadderProfileCache=null;
      if(settled.alreadyAwarded){
        showToast("本場天梯積分先前已完成結算，不會重複加分");
      }else if(state.ladderSkippedPlayers.length){
        showToast("天梯積分結算完成；"+state.ladderSkippedPlayers.length+" 位測試／未綁定玩家已略過");
      }else{
        showToast("天梯積分結算完成");
      }
    }catch(e){
      console.error("[ladder settlement]",e);
      const raw=String((e&&e.message)||"");
      const known={"admin-required":"只有管理員／最高管理員可以結算正式積分賽","inactive-account":"目前帳號已停用，無法結算","auth-required":"登入狀態已失效，請重新登入","service-unavailable":"積分結算服務尚未部署"};
      const key=Object.keys(known).find(k=>raw.includes(k));
      state.settlementError=key?known[key]:(raw&&raw!=="request-failed"?"天梯
--- match 5 @ 1123552 ---
gistration({action:'mine',code});
          tournamentDetailMyRegs=(mine?.rows||[]).filter(row=>row&&['confirmed','waitlist','pending_draw'].includes(row.status));
          tournamentDetailMyReg=tournamentDetailMyRegs.find(row=>!row.familyPlayerId)||tournamentDetailMyRegs[0]||null;
          familyLoaded=true;
        }catch(e){console.warn('[BXH family registration mine failed; fallback to legacy registration read]',e)}
      }
      if(!familyLoaded&&window.cloudSync.getMyRegistrationForTournament){
        tournamentDetailMyReg=await window.cloudSync.getMyRegistrationForTournament(code);
        tournamentDetailMyRegs=tournamentDetailMyReg?[tournamentDetailMyReg]:[];
      }
    }
    tournamentDetailError = null;
  }catch(e){
    tournamentDetailError = mapRegistrationError(e);
  }
  tournamentDetailLoading = false;
  render();
}

function resetTournamentDetailState(){
  tournamentDetailCode = null; tournamentDetailData = null; tournamentDetailMyReg = null; tournamentDetailMyRegs = [];
  tournamentDetailLoading = false; tournamentDetailError = null;
  tournamentDetailChildConfirmed = false; tournamentDetailBusy = false;
}

function renderTournamentDetailScreen(){
  const code = tournamentDetailCode;
  if(tournamentDetailLoading || !tournamentDetailData){
    return authShellOpen() + `
      ${authBrandHeader()}
      <div class="btn-row" style="margin:12px 0;"><button class="btn btn-ghost" data-action="back-to-find-events">← 返回賽事列表</button></div>
      <div class="panel"><div class="empty-state">${tournamentDetailError?esc(tournamentDetailError):"正在讀取賽事資訊……"}</div></div>
      ${tournamentDetailError ? `<div class="btn-row" style="justify-content:center;"><button class="btn btn-ghost" data-action="reload-tournament-detail" data-code="${esc(code||'')}">重新整理</button></div>` : ""}
    ${renderModal()}
  </div>`;
  }
  const t = tournamentDetailData;
  const now = Date.now();
  const effStatus = getEffectiveRegistrationStatus(Object.assign({}, t, {
    registrationStatus: t.eventCancelled ? "cancelled" : t.registrationStatus
  }), now);
  const confirmedRemaining = t.registrationSelection ? Math.max(0,t.registrationSelection.capacity-(t.confirmedCount||0)) : Math.max(0, (t.capacity||0) - (t.confirmedCount||0));
  const waitlistRemaining = Math.max(0, (t.waitlistCapacity||0) - (t.waitlistCount||0));
  const isGuestMode=isGuestReadOnlyContext();
  const activeMyRegs=isGuestMode?[]:(Array.isArray(tournamentDetailMyRegs)&&tournamentDetailMyRegs.length?tournamentDetailMyRegs:(tournamentDetailMyReg?[tournamentDetailMyReg]:[])).filter(row=>row&&['confirmed','waitlist','pending_draw'].includes(row.status));
  const selfReg=activeMyRegs.find(row=>!row.familyPlayerId)||null;
  const myReg=selfReg||activeMyRegs[0]||null;
  const hasSelfRegistration=!!selfReg;
  const isLoggedIn=!!(userProfile&&firebaseUser);
  const profileIncomplete=isLoggedIn&&!userProfile.realName;
  const childRegistrationAllowed=!t.registrationSelection&&!(t.ladderMode==='ranked'&&!t.testLadderEnabled);
  const additionalRegistrationAvailable=confirmedRemaining>0||(t.waitlistEnabled&&waitlistRemaining>0);

  let actionHtml;
  if(publicTournamentRegistrationLocked(t)){
    actionHtml = `<div class="banner warn"><span>賽事已開始，報名、備取與取消報名功能均已鎖定。</span></div>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" data-action="switch-to-player-watch" data-code="${esc(code)}">${t.tournamentPhase==="done"?"查看比賽結果":"查看即時賽況"}</button>`;
  } else if(isGuestMode){
    actionHtml = `<div class="hint">遊客模式僅供瀏覽公開賽事資訊，不提供報名／取消報名操作。</div>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" data-action="select-role-player">切換至玩家模式</button>`;
  } else if(activeMyRegs.length){
    const canCancel=t.cancellationDeadline&&now<t.cancellationDeadline&&t.tournamentPhase==="waiting";
    const requiresCheckin=t.checkinRequired===true||t.parsedData?.meta?.checkinRequired===true;
    const participantCards=activeMyRegs.map(row=>{
      const statusLabel=t.registrationSelection?.status==='drawing'?'抽籤處理中':t.registrationSelection?.status==='open'||row.status==='pending_draw'?'待抽籤':row.status==='confirmed'?'正取':'備取'+(row.waitRank?'第 '+row.waitRank+' 位（原始順位）':'');
      const participantName=row.participantName||row.displayName||row.realName||(!row.familyPlayerId?(userProfile?.displayName||userProfile?.realName):'家庭選手')||'參賽者';
      return '<div class="stat-box"><div class="label">'+esc(participantName)+'</div><div class="value small">'+esc(statusLabel)+'</div></div>';
    }).join('');
    const addSelfButton=additionalRegistrationAvailable&&t.targetGroup!=="children"&&!hasSelfRegistration?`<button class="btn btn-primary" data-action="submit-tournament-registration" data-participant-mode="self" data-code="${esc(code)}" ${tournamentDetailBusy?'disabled':''}>本人報名</button>`:'';
    const addChildButton=additionalRegistrationAvailable&&childRegistrationAllowed?`<button class="btn btn-ghost" data-action="submit-tournament-registration" data-participant-mode="children" data-code="${esc(code)}" ${tournamentDetailBusy?'disabled':''}>兒童報名</button>`:'';
    actionHtml=`
      <div id="registration-result-panel" class="registration-result-panel">
        <div class="registration-result-head"><span class="registration-result-icon">✓</span><div><div class="registration-result-title">我的報名（${activeMyRegs.length}）</div><div class="registration-result-subtitle">同一會員帳號下，每位參賽者各自保留獨立名額與狀態。</div></div></div>
        <div class="registration-result-grid">${participantCards}</div>
        ${requiresCheckin?`<div class="registration-next-step"><b>下一步｜現場繳費與報到</b><br>活動當日請向工作人員完成繳費；每位參賽者都要由工作人員確認報到後，才會正式進入對戰名單。</div>`:''}
        ${t.targetGroup==="children"&&addChildButton?`<label class="checkbox-row"><input type="checkbox" id="child-eligibility-checkbox" ${tournamentDetailChildConfirmed?'checked':''}> 我確認報名兒童符合 6 歲以上、13 歲以下的參加資格</label>`:''}
        ${addSelfButton||addChildButton?`<div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:12px;">${addSelfButton}${addChildButton}</div>`:''}
        <div class="registration-cancel-row">${canCancel?`<button class="btn btn-sm registration-cancel-link" data-action="cancel-my-registration" data-code="${esc(code)}" ${tournamentDetailBusy?'disabled':''}>${activeMyRegs.length>1?'取消其中一位報名':'取消這場報名'}</button>`:`<span class="hint">${t.cancellationDeadline?"已超過取消期限，無法取消":"目前無法取消報名"}</span>`}</div>
      </div>

  } else if(effStatus==="cancelled" || t.eventCancelled){

function renderLadderSeasonHistory(p){
  const h=p&&p.seasonHistory||{};
  const keys=Object.keys(h).sort((a,b)=>Number(String(b).replace(/\D/g,""))-Number(String(a).replace(/\D/g,"")));
  if(!keys.length) return '<div class="empty-state">尚無已結束賽季紀錄</div>';
  return `<div class="rank-scroll"><table class="rank-table"><thead><tr><th>賽季</th><th>最終積分</th><th>最終階級</th><th>最終排名</th></tr></thead><tbody>${keys.map(k=>{const x=h[k]||{};return `<tr><td>${esc(k)}</td><td>${Number(x.finalPoints||0)} PT</td><td>${esc(x.finalRankTier||"—")}</td><td>${x.finalRanking?`NO.${x.finalRanking}`:"—"}</td></tr>`;}).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`;
}
function renderLadderTransactionList(logs){
  if(!logs||!logs.length) return '<div class="empty-state">尚無積分紀錄</div>';
  return `<div class="rank-scroll"><table class="rank-table"><thead><tr><th>時間</th><th>玩家</th><th>類型</th><th>異動</th><th>賽季</th><th>原因／賽事</th><th>操作人</th></tr></thead><tbody>${logs.slice(0,100).map(x=>`<tr><td>${x.createdAtMs?new Date(x.createdAtMs).toLocaleString():"—"}</td><td>${esc(x.playerName||"—")}</td><td>${x.type==="event"?"賽事積分":x.type==="adjustment"?"手動更正":esc(x.type||"—")}</td><td>${Number(x.delta||x.pointsEarned||0)>=0?"+":""}${Number(x.delta||x.pointsEarned||0)}</td><td>${esc(x.seasonId||"—")}</td><td>${esc(x.reason||x.eventName||"—")+(x.scoring?`<br><small>V1｜${Number(x.scoring.effectiveCount)||0} 人 ×${Number(x.scoring.multiplier).toFixed(3)}｜${Number(x.scoring.basePoints)||0}＋${Number(x.scoring.weightedBonus)||0} 分</small>`:"")}</td><td>${esc(x.actorName||x.actorUid||"—")}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`;
}

function deriveDoubleElimPlacements(st){
  const champ=st.championId, runner=st.runnerUpId;
  const elimination=[];
  const lossCounts={};
  (st.matches||[]).filter(m=>!m.isBye&&m.completed&&m.loserId).sort((a,b)=>(a.seq||0)-(b.seq||0)).forEach(m=>{
    lossCounts[m.loserId]=(lossCounts[m.loserId]||0)+1;
    if(lossCounts[m.loserId]>=2) elimination.push({id:m.loserId,seq:Number(m.seq||0)});
  });
  const rest=elimination.filter(x=>x.id!==runner&&x.id!==champ).sort((a,b)=>b.seq-a.seq);
  return { championId:champ, runnerUpId:runner, thirdId:rest[0]&&rest[0].id||null, fourthId:rest[1]&&rest[1].id||null };
}
function buildLadderSettlementPayload(st){
  if(st&&st.meta&&st.meta.eventAuthority==="community") return {eventCode:st.cloudCode,eventName:(st.meta&&st.meta.name)||"",eventDate:(st.meta&&st.meta.date)||"",formatType:(st.meta&&st.meta.formatType)||"single",participants:[]};
  let places={championId:st.championId,runnerUpId:st.runnerUpId,thirdId:st.thirdId,fourthId:st.fourthId};
  if((st.meta&&st.meta.formatType)==="double") places=deriveDoubleElimPlacements(st);
  const standings=st.meta?.formatType==='roundrobin'?computeRoundRobinStandings(st):[];
  const scored=window.BXHLadderV1.analyze(st,places,standings);
  const participants=scored.players.map(({player:p,placement,pointsEarned,wins,losses,scoring})=>{
    return { localPlayerId:p.id, testPlayerKey:p.testPlayerKey||null, playerUid:p.familyPlayerId||p.registrationUid||p.accountUid||p.uid||null, playerId:p.playerId||null, playerName:p.name, source:p.familyPlayerId?"family":p.source||"onsite", participantType:p.participantType||null, accountLinked:p.accountLinked===true, ladderEligible:p.ladderEligible!==false, activityEligible:p.activityEligible!==false, placement, pointsEarned, wins, losses, scoring };
  });
  return { eventCode:st.cloudCode, eventName:(st.meta&&st.meta.name)||"", eventDate:(st.meta&&st.meta.date)||"", formatType:(st.meta&&st.meta.formatType)||"single", scoringVersion:window.BXHLadderV1.version, effectiveCount:scored.count, multiplier:scored.weight, participants };
}

function playerDerivedId(p){
  if(!p) return "—";
  if(p.playerId) return p.playerId;
  const uid = p.uid || (firebaseUser && firebaseUser.uid) || "";
  return uid ? ("P"+uid.slice(-6).toUpperCase()) : "—";
}

  </nav>`;
}

let mailboxState=null;
function mailboxContext(){
  const key=currentAuthUid()+':'+engagementSessionEpoch;
  if(!mailboxState||mailboxState.key!==key)mailboxState={key,open:false,loading:false,busy:false,messages:null,unreadCount:0,selectedId:'',error:''};
  return mailboxState;
}
function mailboxDate(value){
  if(!value)return '—';
  try{return new Date(value).toLocaleString('zh-TW',{timeZone:'Asia/Taipei',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});}catch(e){return '—';}
}
function mailboxError(error){
  const raw=String(error?.message||error||'');
  const map={'mail-not-found':'找不到這封信，可能已被移除。','recipient-not-found':'找不到指定玩家帳號。','recipient-inactive':'指定玩家帳號目前不可使用。','super-admin-required':'只有最高管理員可以發送系統信。','invalid-subject':'信件標題請輸入 1～80 字。','invalid-body':'信件內容請輸入 1～2000 字。','operation-conflict':'這次寄送識別碼與先前內容不一致，請重新操作。','auth-required':'請重新登入後再查看站內信。','service-unavailable':'站內信服務尚未部署。'};
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(map))if(raw.includes(key))return value;
  return '站內信暫時無法連線，請稍後重試。';
}
async function loadMailbox(force=false){
  const c=mailboxContext();
  if(!firebaseUser?.uid||c.loading||c.busy||(!force&&Array.isArray(c.messages)))return;
  c.loading=true;c.error='';render();
  try{
    if(!window.engagementService?.mailbox)throw Error('service-unavailable');
    const result=await window.engagementService.mailbox({action:'list'});
    if(c!==mailboxContext())return;
    if(!result?.ok)throw Error('load-failed');
    c.messages=Array.isArray(result.messages)?result.messages:[];
    c.unreadCount=Math.max(0,Number(result.unreadCount)||0);
    if(c.selectedId&&!c.messages.some(m=>m.id===c.selectedId))c.selectedId='';
  }catch(error){if(c===mailboxContext())c.error=mailboxError(error);}
  finally{if(c===mailboxContext()){c.loading=false;render();}}
}
function mailboxButtonHtml(){
  const c=mailboxContext();
  return `<button class="mailbox-trigger ${c.open?'active':''}" data-action="mailbox-open" aria-label="站內信${c.unreadCount?'，'+c.unreadCount+'封未讀':''}"><span aria-hidden="true">✉️</span><span class="mailbox-label">站內信</span>${c.unreadCount?`<span class="mailbox-unread">${c.unreadCount>99?'99+':c.unreadCount}</span>`:''}</button>`;
}

    state.players=selected.map(name=>({

      name,
      testPlayerKey:"T"+String(BXH_TEST_PLAYER_POOL.indexOf(name)+1).padStart(3,"0"),
      checkedIn:true,
      source:"test"
    }));
    state.testDataDismissed=true;
    state.meta.checkinClosedEarly=false;
    state.meta.checkinClosedAt=null;

    const ta = document.getElementById("quick-add-textarea");
    const lines = ta.value.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/).map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if(lines.length===0){ showToast("請至少輸入一位選手", true); return; }
    const defaultChecked = !state.meta.checkinRequired;

        participantType:"onsite_guest",

      showToast("目前報到已鎖定，無法變更報到狀態",true);
      return;

      p.checkedIn = true;
      saveState(); render();
      showToast("「"+p.name+"」已完成報到");
      return;

    openModal({
      type:"generic",
      title:"是否要取消報到？",
      message:"「"+p.name+"」目前為已報到狀態。是否要取消報到？",
      danger:true,
      confirmLabel:"是",
      cancelLabel:"否",

    async getPlayerBadges(uids){const call=fnx.httpsCallable(functionsHandle,'playerCardService',{timeout:15000});const r=await call({action:'badges',uids});return r.data;},
    async getLadderSnapshot(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return {control:null,season:null,players:[]};

      // Control and player collection are independent: fetch them in parallel.
      // Previously these were serial, followed by another season read, causing
      // avoidable latency on mobile even with only one ladder player.
      const [controlSnap,ps]=await Promise.all([
        fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current")),
        fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers"))
      ]);
      const control=controlSnap.exists()?controlSnap.data():null;
      const players=[]; ps.forEach(d=>players.push(Object.assign({uid:d.id},d.data())));

      let season=null;
      if(control&&control.currentSeason){
        const ss=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSeasons",control.currentSeason));
        if(ss.exists()) season=ss.data();
      }
      return {control,season,players};
    },

    async getLadderPlayer(uid){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!uid) return null;
      const s=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers",uid));
      return s.exists()?Object.assign({uid:s.id},s.data()):null;
    },

    async getLadderTransactions(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return [];
      const s=await fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderTransactions"));
      const out=[]; s.forEach(d=>{const x=Object.assign({id:d.id},d.data()); x.createdAtMs=cloudSafeTimestampMs(x.createdAt); out.push(x);});
      return out.sort((a,b)=>(b.createdAtMs||0)-(a.createdAtMs||0)).slice(0,200);
    },

    async getTestLadderRanking(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return [];
      const snap=await fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"testLadderPlayers"));
      const out=[]; snap.forEach(d=>out.push(Object.assign({testPlayerKey:d.id},d.data())));
      return out.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.seasonPoints||0)-Number(a.seasonPoints||0))||(Number(b.wins||0)-Number(a.wins||0))||String(a.testPlayerKey).localeCompare(String(b.testPlayerKey)));
    },

    async settleTestLadderTournament(code,payload){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!code) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      const eventCode=String(code).toUpperCase();
      const tournamentSnap=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"tournaments",eventCode));
      if(!tournamentSnap.exists()) return {ok:false,reason:"event-not-found"};
      const tournament=tournamentSnap.data()||{};
      if(tournament.testMode!==true||tournament.eventAuthority!=="test"||tournament.ownerUid!==actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"not-own-test-event",message:"只能結算自己建立的測試賽事"};
      const items=(payload&&payload.participants||[]).filter(x=>x&&x.source==="test"&&/^T\d{3}$/.test(String(x.testPlayerKey||"")));
      if(!items.length) return {ok:true,skipped:true,reason:"no-test-players",message:"本場皆為現場新增選手，未計入 TEST-S1；僅 T001～T064 實驗玩家可累積測試積分",results:[],skippedPlayers:(payload?.participants||[]).map(p=>p.playerName)};
      const results=[]; let newWrites=0;
      for(const item of items){
        const key=String(item.testPlayerKey);
        const txId=eventCode+"_"+key;
        const txRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"testLadderTransactions",txId);
        const playerRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"testLadderPlayers",key);
        const r=await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
          const prior=await tx.get(txRef);
          if(prior.exists()) return {already:true,points:Number(prior.data().delta||0)};
          const ps=await tx.get(playerRef);
          const old=ps.exists()?ps.data():{};
          const delta=Number(item.pointsEarned||0);
          const next={
            testPlayerKey:key, playerName:String(item.playerName||key), seasonId:"TEST-S1",
            seasonPoints:Number(old.seasonPoints||0)+delta, careerPoints:Number(old.careerPoints||0)+delta,
            totalEvents:Number(old.totalEvents||0)+1, wins:Number(old.wins||0)+Number(item.wins||0), losses:Number(old.losses||0)+Number(item.losses||0),
            championCount:Number(old.championCount||0)+(item.placement===1?1:0), runnerUpCount:Number(old.runnerUpCount||0)+(item.placement===2?1:0),
            thirdPlaceCount:Number(old.thirdPlaceCount||0)+(item.placement===3?1:0), fourthPlaceCount:Number(old.fourthPlaceCount||0)+(item.placement===4?1:0),
            updatedAt:Date.now()
          };
          tx.set(playerRef,next,{merge:true});
          tx.set(txRef,{type:"event",eventAuthority:"test",actorUid,testPlayerKey:key,playerName:String(item.playerName||key),seasonId:"TEST-S1",delta,eventCode,eventName:String(payload.eventName||""),placement:item.placement||null,wins:Number(item.wins||0),losses:Number(item.losses||0),createdAt:Date.now()});
          return {already:false,points:delta};
        });
        if(!r.already) newWrites++;
        results.push({testPlayerKey:key,playerName:item.playerName,pointsEarned:r.points,placement:item.placement||null,alreadyAwarded:!!r.already});
      }
      return {ok:true,seasonId:"TEST-S1",awardedAt:Date.now(),alreadyAwarded:newWrites===0,results};
    },

    async settleLadderTournament(code,payload){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!code) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};

    async adjustLadderPoints(uid,newSeasonPoints,reason,actorName){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!uid||!reason) return {ok:false,reason:"invalid"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      const playerRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers",uid);
      const controlRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current");
      const logRef=fx.doc(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderTransactions"));
      return await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
        const ps=await tx.get(playerRef), cs=await tx.get(controlRef);
        if(!ps.exists()) return {ok:false,reason:"not-found"};
        if(!cs.exists()||cs.data().rolloverInProgress===true) return {ok:false,reason:"season-unavailable"};
        const control=cs.data(); const isTrialSeason=String(control.currentSeason||"").toUpperCase()==="S0"||control.isTrialSeason===true;
        const old=ps.data(); const before=Math.max(0,Number(old.seasonPoints||0)); const after=Math.max(0,Number(newSeasonPoints)||0); const delta=after-before;
        const beforeCareer=Math.max(0,Number(old.careerPoints||0)); const afterCareer=isTrialSeason?beforeCareer:Math.max(0,beforeCareer+delta);
        const rankTier=cloudLadderTier(after); const oldHigh=old.highestRankTier||"黑鐵"; const highest=isTrialSeason?oldHigh:(cloudLadderTierIndex(rankTier)>cloudLadderTierIndex(oldHigh)?rankTier:oldHigh);
        tx.update(playerRef,{seasonPoints:after,careerPoints:afterCareer,rankTier,highestRankTier:highest,lastPointsUpdatedAt:fx.serverTimestamp()});
        tx.set(logRef,{type:"adjustment",playerUid:uid,playerName:old.playerName||"",seasonId:old.currentSeason||control.currentSeason||"S1",isTrialSeason,countsTowardCareer:!isTrialSeason,beforeSeasonPoints:before,afterSeasonPoints:after,beforeCareerPoints:beforeCareer,afterCareerPoints:afterCareer,delta,reason,actorUid,actorName:actorName||"",createdAt:fx.serverTimestamp()});
        return {ok:true,before,after,delta};
      });
    },

      if(tournament.testMode!==true||tournament.eventAuthority!=="test"||tournament.ownerUid!==actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"not-own-test-event",message:"只能結算自己建立的測試賽事"};
      const items=(payload&&payload.participants||[]).filter(x=>x&&x.source==="test"&&/^T\d{3}$/.test(String(x.testPlayerKey||"")));
      if(!items.length) return {ok:true,skipped:true,reason:"no-test-players",message:"本場皆為現場新增選手，未計入 TEST-S1；僅 T001～T064 實驗玩家可累積測試積分",results:[],skippedPlayers:(payload?.participants||[]).map(p=>p.playerName)};
      const results=[]; let newWrites=0;
      for(const item of items){
        const key=String(item.testPlayerKey);
        const txId=eventCode+"_"+key;
        const txRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"testLadderTransactions",txId);
        const playerRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"testLadderPlayers",key);
        const r=await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
          const prior=await tx.get(txRef);
          if(prior.exists()) return {already:true,points:Number(prior.data().delta||0)};
          const ps=await tx.get(playerRef);
          const old=ps.exists()?ps.data():{};
          const delta=Number(item.pointsEarned||0);
          const next={
            testPlayerKey:key, playerName:String(item.playerName||key), seasonId:"TEST-S1",
            seasonPoints:Number(old.seasonPoints||0)+delta, careerPoints:Number(old.careerPoints||0)+delta,
            totalEvents:Number(old.totalEvents||0)+1, wins:Number(old.wins||0)+Number(item.wins||0), losses:Number(old.losses||0)+Number(item.losses||0),
            championCount:Number(old.championCount||0)+(item.placement===1?1:0), runnerUpCount:Number(old.runnerUpCount||0)+(item.placement===2?1:0),
            thirdPlaceCount:Number(old.thirdPlaceCount||0)+(item.placement===3?1:0), fourthPlaceCount:Number(old.fourthPlaceCount||0)+(item.placement===4?1:0),
            updatedAt:Date.now()
          };
          tx.set(playerRef,next,{merge:true});
          tx.set(txRef,{type:"event",eventAuthority:"test",actorUid,testPlayerKey:key,playerName:String(item.playerName||key),seasonId:"TEST-S1",delta,eventCode,eventName:String(payload.eventName||""),placement:item.placement||null,wins:Number(item.wins||0),losses:Number(item.losses||0),createdAt:Date.now()});
          return {already:false,points:delta};
        });
        if(!r.already) newWrites++;
        results.push({testPlayerKey:key,playerName:item.playerName,pointsEarned:r.points,placement:item.placement||null,alreadyAwarded:!!r.already});
      }
      return {ok:true,seasonId:"TEST-S1",awardedAt:Date.now(),alreadyAwarded:newWrites===0,results};
    },

        if(!cs.exists()||cs.data().rolloverInProgress===true) return {ok:false,reason:"season-unavailable"};

function renderLadderSeasonHistory(p){
  const h=p&&p.seasonHistory||{};
  const keys=Object.keys(h).sort((a,b)=>Number(String(b).replace(/\D/g,""))-Number(String(a).replace(/\D/g,"")));
  if(!keys.length) return '<div class="empty-state">尚無已結束賽季紀錄</div>';
  return `<div class="rank-scroll"><table class="rank-table"><thead><tr><th>賽季</th><th>最終積分</th><th>最終階級</th><th>最終排名</th></tr></thead><tbody>${keys.map(k=>{const x=h[k]||{};return `<tr><td>${esc(k)}</td><td>${Number(x.finalPoints||0)} PT</td><td>${esc(x.finalRankTier||"—")}</td><td>${x.finalRanking?`NO.${x.finalRanking}`:"—"}</td></tr>`;}).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`;
}
function renderLadderTransactionList(logs){
  if(!logs||!logs.length) return '<div class="empty-state">尚無積分紀錄</div>';
  return `<div class="rank-scroll"><table class="rank-table"><thead><tr><th>時間</th><th>玩家</th><th>類型</th><th>異動</th><th>賽季</th><th>原因／賽事</th><th>操作人</th></tr></thead><tbody>${logs.slice(0,100).map(x=>`<tr><td>${x.createdAtMs?new Date(x.createdAtMs).toLocaleString():"—"}</td><td>${esc(x.playerName||"—")}</td><td>${x.type==="event"?"賽事積分":x.type==="adjustment"?"手動更正":esc(x.type||"—")}</td><td>${Number(x.delta||x.pointsEarned||0)>=0?"+":""}${Number(x.delta||x.pointsEarned||0)}</td><td>${esc(x.seasonId||"—")}</td><td>${esc(x.reason||x.eventName||"—")+(x.scoring?`<br><small>V1｜${Number(x.scoring.effectiveCount)||0} 人 ×${Number(x.scoring.multiplier).toFixed(3)}｜${Number(x.scoring.basePoints)||0}＋${Number(x.scoring.weightedBonus)||0} 分</small>`:"")}</td><td>${esc(x.actorName||x.actorUid||"—")}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`;
}

function deriveDoubleElimPlacements(st){
  const champ=st.championId, runner=st.runnerUpId;
  const elimination=[];
  const lossCounts={};
  (st.matches||[]).filter(m=>!m.isBye&&m.completed&&m.loserId).sort((a,b)=>(a.seq||0)-(b.seq||0)).forEach(m=>{
    lossCounts[m.loserId]=(lossCounts[m.loserId]||0)+1;
    if(lossCounts[m.loserId]>=2) elimination.push({id:m.loserId,seq:Number(m.seq||0)});
  });
  const rest=elimination.filter(x=>x.id!==runner&&x.id!==champ).sort((a,b)=>b.seq-a.seq);
  return { championId:champ, runnerUpId:runner, thirdId:rest[0]&&rest[0].id||null, fourthId:rest[1]&&rest[1].id||null };
}
function buildLadderSettlementPayload(st){
  if(st&&st.meta&&st.meta.eventAuthority==="community") return {eventCode:st.cloudCode,eventName:(st.meta&&st.meta.name)||"",eventDate:(st.meta&&st.meta.date)||"",formatType:(st.meta&&st.meta.formatType)||"single",participants:[]};
  let places={championId:st.championId,runnerUpId:st.runnerUpId,thirdId:st.thirdId,fourthId:st.fourthId};
  if((st.meta&&st.meta.formatType)==="double") places=deriveDoubleElimPlacements(st);
  const standings=st.meta?.formatType==='roundrobin'?computeRoundRobinStandings(st):[];
  const scored=window.BXHLadderV1.analyze(st,places,standings);
  const participants=scored.players.map(({player:p,placement,pointsEarned,wins,losses,scoring})=>{
    return { localPlayerId:p.id, testPlayerKey:p.testPlayerKey||null, playerUid:p.familyPlayerId||p.registrationUid||p.accountUid||p.uid||null, playerId:p.playerId||null, playerName:p.name, source:p.familyPlayerId?"family":p.source||"onsite", participantType:p.participantType||null, accountLinked:p.accountLinked===true, ladderEligible:p.ladderEligible!==false, activityEligible:p.activityEligible!==false, placement, pointsEarned, wins, losses, scoring };
  });
  return { eventCode:st.cloudCode, eventName:(st.meta&&st.meta.name)||"", eventDate:(st.meta&&st.meta.date)||"", formatType:(st.meta&&st.meta.formatType)||"single", scoringVersion:window.BXHLadderV1.version, effectiveCount:scored.count, multiplier:scored.weight, participants };
}

function playerDerivedId(p){
  if(!p) return "—";
  if(p.playerId) return p.playerId;
  const uid = p.uid || (firebaseUser && firebaseUser.uid) || "";
  return uid ? ("P"+uid.slice(-6).toUpperCase()) : "—";
}

function renderPlayerNavHtml(){
  return `<nav class="player-tabs">
    ${PLAYER_TABS.map(([key,label])=>`<button class="player-tab-btn ${playerActiveTab===key?'active':''}" data-action="player-switch-tab" data-tab="${key}">${esc(label)}</button>`).join("")}
  </nav>`;
}

let mailboxState=null;
function mailboxContext(){
  const key=currentAuthUid()+':'+engagementSessionEpoch;
  if(!mailboxState||mailboxState.key!==key)mailboxState={key,open:false,loading:false,busy:false,messages:null,unreadCount:0,selectedId:'',error:''};
  return mailboxState;
}
function mailboxDate(value){
  if(!value)return '—';
  try{return new Date(value).toLocaleString('zh-TW',{timeZone:'Asia/Taipei',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});}catch(e){return '—';}
}
function mailboxError(error){
  const raw=String(error?.message||error||'');
  const map={'mail-not-found':'找不到這封信，可能已被移除。','recipient-not-found':'找不到指定玩家帳號。','recipient-inactive':'指定玩家帳號目前不可使用。','super-admin-required':'只有最高管理員可以發送系統信。','invalid-subject':'信件標題請輸入 1～80 字。','invalid-body':'信件內容請輸入 1～2000 字。','operation-conflict':'這次寄送識別碼與先前內容不一致，請重新操作。','auth-required':'請重新登入後再查看站內信。','service-unavailable':'站內信服務尚未部署。'};
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(map))if(raw.includes(key))return value;
  return '站內信暫時無法連線，請稍後重試。';
}
async function loadMailbox(force=false){
  const c=mailboxContext();
  if(!firebaseUser?.uid||c.loading||c.busy||(!force&&Array.isArray(c.messages)))return;
  c.loading=true;c.error='';render();
  try{
    if(!window.engagementService?.mailbox)throw Error('service-unavailable');
    const result=await window.engagementService.mailbox({action:'list'});
    if(c!==mailboxContext())return;
    if(!result?.ok)throw Error('load-failed');
    c.messages=Array.isArray(result.messages)?result.messages:[];
    c.unreadCount=Math.max(0,Number(result.unreadCount)||0);
    if(c.selectedId&&!c.messages.some(m=>m.id===c.selectedId))c.selectedId='';
  }catch(error){if(c===mailboxContext())c.error=mailboxError(error);}
  finally{if(c===mailboxContext()){c.loading=false;render();}}
}
function mailboxButtonHtml(){
  const c=mailboxContext();
  return `<button class="mailbox-trigger ${c.open?'active':''}" data-action="mailbox-open" aria-label="站內信${c.unreadCount?'，'+c.unreadCount+'封未讀':''}"><span aria-hidden="true">✉️</span><span class="mailbox-label">站內信</span>${c.unreadCount?`<span class="mailbox-unread">${c.unreadCount>99?'99+':c.unreadCount}</span>`:''}</button>`;
}
function renderMailboxPage(){
  const c=mailboxContext(),messages=c.messages||[],selected=messages.find(m=>m.id===c.selectedId)||null;
  return `<section class="panel"><div class="panel-title"><span>✉️ 站內信</span><div class="btn-row"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="mailbox-refresh" ${c.loading||c.busy?'disabled':''}>重新整理</button><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="mailbox-close">返回賽事大廳</button></div></div>
    <p class="hint">未讀 ${c.unreadCount} 封｜系統通知、活動及中獎訊息會集中在這裡。</p>
    ${c.error?`<div class="auth-error" role="alert">${esc(c.error)}</div>`:''}

    async getPlayerBadges(uids){const call=fnx.httpsCallable(functionsHandle,'playerCardService',{timeout:15000});const r=await call({action:'badges',uids});return r.data;},
    async getLadderSnapshot(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return {control:null,season:null,players:[]};

      // Control and player collection are independent: fetch them in parallel.
      // Previously these were serial, followed by another season read, causing
      // avoidable latency on mobile even with only one ladder player.
      const [controlSnap,ps]=await Promise.all([
        fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current")),
        fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers"))
      ]);
      const control=controlSnap.exists()?controlSnap.data():null;
      const players=[]; ps.forEach(d=>players.push(Object.assign({uid:d.id},d.data())));

      let season=null;
      if(control&&control.currentSeason){
        const ss=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSeasons",control.currentSeason));
        if(ss.exists()) season=ss.data();
      }
      return {control,season,players};
    },

    async getLadderPlayer(uid){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!uid) return null;
      const s=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers",uid));
      return s.exists()?Object.assign({uid:s.id},s.data()):null;
    },

    async getLadderTransactions(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return [];
      const s=await fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderTransactions"));
      const out=[]; s.forEach(d=>{const x=Object.assign({id:d.id},d.data()); x.createdAtMs=cloudSafeTimestampMs(x.createdAt); out.push(x);});
      return out.sort((a,b)=>(b.createdAtMs||0)-(a.createdAtMs||0)).slice(0,200);
    },

    async getTestLadderRanking(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return [];
      const snap=await fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"testLadderPlayers"));
      const out=[]; snap.forEach(d=>out.push(Object.assign({testPlayerKey:d.id},d.data())));
      return out.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.seasonPoints||0)-Number(a.seasonPoints||0))||(Number(b.wins||0)-Number(a.wins||0))||String(a.testPlayerKey).localeCompare(String(b.testPlayerKey)));
    },

    async settleTestLadderTournament(code,payload){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!code) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      const eventCode=String(code).toUpperCase();
      const tournamentSnap=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"tournaments",eventCode));
      if(!tournamentSnap.exists()) return {ok:false,reason:"event-not-found"};
      const tournament=tournamentSnap.data()||{};
      if(tournament.testMode!==true||tournament.eventAuthority!=="test"||tournament.ownerUid!==actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"not-own-test-event",message:"只能結算自己建立的測試賽事"};
      const items=(payload&&payload.participants||[]).filter(x=>x&&x.source==="test"&&/^T\d{3}$/.test(String(x.testPlayerKey||"")));
      if(!items.length) return {ok:true,skipped:true,reason:"no-test-players",message:"本場皆為現場新增選手，未計入 TEST-S1；僅 T001～T064 實驗玩家可累積測試積分",results:[],skippedPlayers:(payload?.participants||[]).map(p=>p.playerName)};
      const results=[]; let newWrites=0;
      for(const item of items){
        const key=String(item.testPlayerKey);
        const txId=eventCode+"_"+key;
        const txRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"testLadderTransactions",txId);
        const playerRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"testLadderPlayers",key);
        const r=await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
          const prior=await tx.get(txRef);
          if(prior.exists()) return {already:true,points:Number(prior.data().delta||0)};
          const ps=await tx.get(playerRef);
          const old=ps.exists()?ps.data():{};
          const delta=Number(item.pointsEarned||0);
          const next={
            testPlayerKey:key, playerName:String(item.playerName||key), seasonId:"TEST-S1",
            seasonPoints:Number(old.seasonPoints||0)+delta, careerPoints:Number(old.careerPoints||0)+delta,
            totalEvents:Number(old.totalEvents||0)+1, wins:Number(old.wins||0)+Number(item.wins||0), losses:Number(old.losses||0)+Number(item.losses||0),
            championCount:Number(old.championCount||0)+(item.placement===1?1:0), runnerUpCount:Number(old.runnerUpCount||0)+(item.placement===2?1:0),
            thirdPlaceCount:Number(old.thirdPlaceCount||0)+(item.placement===3?1:0), fourthPlaceCount:Number(old.fourthPlaceCount||0)+(item.placement===4?1:0),
            updatedAt:Date.now()
          };
          tx.set(playerRef,next,{merge:true});
          tx.set(txRef,{type:"event",eventAuthority:"test",actorUid,testPlayerKey:key,playerName:String(item.playerName||key),seasonId:"TEST-S1",delta,eventCode,eventName:String(payload.eventName||""),placement:item.placement||null,wins:Number(item.wins||0),losses:Number(item.losses||0),createdAt:Date.now()});
          return {already:false,points:delta};
        });
        if(!r.already) newWrites++;
        results.push({testPlayerKey:key,playerName:item.playerName,pointsEarned:r.points,placement:item.placement||null,alreadyAwarded:!!r.already});
      }
      return {ok:true,seasonId:"TEST-S1",awardedAt:Date.now(),alreadyAwarded:newWrites===0,results};
    },

    async settleLadderTournament(code,payload){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!code) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      if(!window.engagementService||typeof window.engagementService.settleLadder!=="function") throw new Error("service-unavailable");
      return window.engagementService.settleLadder({code:String(code).toUpperCase()});

    },

    async adjustLadderPoints(uid,newSeasonPoints,reason,actorName){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!uid||!reason) return {ok:false,reason:"invalid"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      const playerRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers",uid);
      const controlRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current");
      const logRef=fx.doc(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderTransactions"));
      return await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
        const ps=await tx.get(playerRef), cs=await tx.get(controlRef);
        if(!ps.exists()) return {ok:false,reason:"not-found"};
        if(!cs.exists()||cs.data().rolloverInProgress===true) return {ok:false,reason:"season-unavailable"};
        const control=cs.data(); const isTrialSeason=String(control.currentSeason||"").toUpperCase()==="S0"||control.isTrialSeason===true;
        const old=ps.data(); const before=Math.max(0,Number(old.seasonPoints||0)); const after=Math.max(0,Number(newSeasonPoints)||0); const delta=after-before;
        const beforeCareer=Math.max(0,Number(old.careerPoints||0)); const afterCareer=isTrialSeason?beforeCareer:Math.max(0,beforeCareer+delta);
        const rankTier=cloudLadderTier(after); const oldHigh=old.highestRankTier||"黑鐵"; const highest=isTrialSeason?oldHigh:(cloudLadderTierIndex(rankTier)>cloudLadderTierIndex(oldHigh)?rankTier:oldHigh);
        tx.update(playerRef,{seasonPoints:after,careerPoints:afterCareer,rankTier,highestRankTier:highest,lastPointsUpdatedAt:fx.serverTimestamp()});
        tx.set(logRef,{type:"adjustment",playerUid:uid,playerName:old.playerName||"",seasonId:old.currentSeason||control.currentSeason||"S1",isTrialSeason,countsTowardCareer:!isTrialSeason,beforeSeasonPoints:before,afterSeasonPoints:after,beforeCareerPoints:beforeCareer,afterCareerPoints:afterCareer,delta,reason,actorUid,actorName:actorName||"",createdAt:fx.serverTimestamp()});
        return {ok:true,before,after,delta};
      });
    },
